Until then signal types stay one per alias per process.

What breaks

  • Public types removed from the DigitalBrain packages: SynapseHost, SynapseRestorer, SynapseLoader, NeuronSourceStore, LoadedSource, ISourceStore, ISynapseRegistrar, ISynapseRestorer, NoSynapseRestorer, InstallRestore, IInstallsCallbacks - 11 types. SynapseCatalogSource is kept. os references: ISourceStore 4, ISynapseRestorer 1, the rest 0; templates/ and examples/ 0.
  • ISourceStore: 4 os call sites - BuildFlow.cs:477, SynapseStudioNeuron.cs:141 and :168, LegacyRestoreFacts.cs:268 - move to ISynapseHost.Source / List.
  • ISynapseRestorer.TryRestore: 1 os call site (SynapseStudioNeuron.cs:144-148) is deleted, not replaced.
  • ISynapseHost gains Source(brain, sourceKey, ct): 2 os test fakes must implement it (ApprovalsFacts.cs:53 StagedHost, SynapseStudioFacts.cs:66 CountingHost); the 1 production caller (SynapseStudioNeuron.cs:14) compiles unchanged. Deploy/Retire/List signatures, DeployOutcome, DeployedSource, DeployStatus are unchanged.
  • ISynapseCatalogSource: Current is renamed Static on purpose (unit aliases are no longer in it, so a silent keep would make every os 'is it loaded' check answer no); Version, Changed, ForNeeds, Stage, Unstage are removed; Of(Type) is added. os: 5 catalog.Current reads in 3 production files (BrainEventsEndpoints.cs:91, UnitReadEndpoints.cs:44, DebuggerReadsNeuron.cs:84, 110, 114) need the brain's unit catalogs, not only a yes/no; JournalTailEndpointsFacts.cs:27 still compiles (constructor gains an optional parameter). digitalbrain: 14 test files name SynapseCatalogSource.
  • ISources is reshaped (Put/Stage/Remove/Get/List -> Claim/Release/Drained/OwnerOf/Keys/Legacy): 0 os callers. SourceRecord stays as the legacy DTO; os SynapseStudioNeuron.RebuildRequired(SourceRecord) (line 157) and LegacyRestoreFacts.cs:270, 282, 283 change.
  • IInstalls gains Rewire(alias, ct): no implementer in os; 4 digitalbrain test fakes implement IInstalls (HostRefusesBannedApisFacts.cs:333, ResolveRebindFacts.cs:65, SourceBindingsFlipFacts.cs:70, SynapseHostUnwindFacts.cs:86) - two files are deleted, one rewritten, one loses the fake. 9 os files call IInstalls and compile unchanged.
  • DigitalBrain.Testing public surface: TestOS.Synapses changes type from SynapseHost to ISynapseHost (8 test files call Deploy/Retire/List and compile; 5 Leaked assertions in 3 files move to a helper); TestOS.Store, TestOS.Registrar, TestOS.Restorer are removed (3, 2 and 3 digitalbrain test files; 0 os uses); TestOS.Source stays with Static/Merge/Replace/Remove. UnitScenario keeps its public surface (os CircleRoomUnitFacts, MuskXWatchFacts unaffected).
  • Unit boundary: AllowedApis.RefusedDigitalBrainTypes must name every new public DigitalBrain type; forgetting one lets generated code reference deployment plumbing.
  • Persisted data: grain state of {brain}/synapse.sources/default keeps its neuron type and state name; each SourceRecord moves lazily into {brain}/synapse.deployment/{sourceKey} on that deployment's first activation and is then removed from Records (one-way per source). SourceRecord.Bindings is dropped; InstallRecord.Bindings (what BrokerSlotResolver.cs:27-32 reads) is the only copy. InstallsState is unchanged.
  • Journal: synapse.deployed / synapse.staged / synapse.undeployed are published by synapse.deployment/{key} instead of synapse.sources/default; 4 new signal aliases (synapse.stage-changed, synapse.stage-dropped, synapse.aliases-claimed, synapse.aliases-released). No os consumer of the Deployed/Staged/Undeployed signal types was found.
  • Behaviour on purpose: Deploy and Retire no longer block on forced GC, and SynapseHost.Leaked / SynapseLoader.RetryUnload are replaced by UnitLoader.Lingering(); an install failure during a first deploy leaves nothing live; needsDiff compares against the consented needs hash; an approval whose build's needs differ from the asked needs is denied as superseded; List reports RebuildRequired only after a failed resolve recorded it (today it walks every stored PE on every call); the rule 'stored without bindings and declares a slot -> refused' (SynapseRestorer.cs:82-85, os SynapseStudioNeuron.cs:157-158) is gone - such a unit loads and an unbound slot throws SlotUnboundException on use.

Risks

  • Real limit of this move: state, gates, reactions and views become per brain, but unit-declared [Signal] types stay one per alias per process (last loaded wins) until step 5. After R13 two hashes of one unit loaded at once is the normal state, so a typed reaction of the build that lost the map silently does not match (StateNeuron.cs:118-119). If os units declare signals, step 5 is part of this work, not a follow-up.
  • Interleaved reads: Ref/Build/Describe interleave with a Propose turn. Safe only because every transition is a single Save and the neuron never mutates state outside Save; needs a silo fact that reads Ref(Proposed) from inside IInstalls.Install.
  • Promote spans three neurons (installs, deployment, sources). Only the deployment's own transition is atomic; a crash between its appends is repaired by promote-on-activate or by deploying again, not by a transaction.
  • State size: every Save rewrites the whole DeploymentState, which holds up to two images (PE + PDB + files, base64), and Propose saves 2-3 times. It is still smaller per write than today's SourcesState, which holds every source of the brain in one record, but the number of writes per deploy goes up.
  • Latency: a build with no activation is unloaded; the next activation costs two read-only neuron calls plus LoadFromStream, and 'deploy, then first call' loads the build twice because UnitDeployer releases its handle when Deploy returns.
  • LiveBindings is a per-silo hint. It is fed by Promote on the deployment's silo and by a Deployed/Undeployed listener on the others (ASSUMPTION: IClusterListeners reaches every silo); where it is not fed, an old activation keeps serving the old build - consistently, with its own functions - until it goes idle.
  • Routing: an unknown neuron type now reaches neuron.host and fails in OnActivateAsync after one or two neuron reads instead of failing at the caller. A typo costs an activation attempt.
  • The legacy drain is one-way per source: after Drained, a rolled-back os build no longer finds that record. Back up synapse.sources grain state before the os deploy.
  • TestOS.Host(NeuronId) is synchronous, so one sync-over-async resolve survives there until TestOS activation becomes async (R14).
  • Brain-wide edge fan-out: every deployment neuron of a brain receives every Installed/InstallDenied of that brain; each non-matching delivery is one pure comparison, but it activates the neuron.
  • Step 3 is one large PR (about 1,800 lines out, 1,000 in, some 20 test files touched). It cannot be split without writing a throwaway registrar shim that would itself need the cross-brain refcount.

Proof it worked

New facts: DeploymentStateFacts (every transition, no runtime); StagedUpdateSurvivesRestartFacts on TestOS.Restart() and on a silo restart (stage, restart, approve -> the new hash is live and Installed carries its needs hash; update with changed needs after a restart is Staged, not Deployed; a ready stage left by a crash is promoted on activation); TwoBrainsRunDifferentVersionsFacts on TestOS and on a silo (A swaps, B's gate still refuses with its own rule and its view still renders its own version, A retires, B unaffected); AnActivationKeepsItsOwnFunctionsWhenItsAliasIsReplaced on a silo (step 2); AliasClaimSurvivesRestartFacts (deploy A with alias x, restart, deploy B with alias x -> Refused); LegacySourcesMigrateFacts (pre-R13 SourcesState JSON -> first call runs the unit, Records empty, Owners filled); UnitLoaderFacts (a disallowed image never loads; release at zero unloads; Lingering reaches 0 after a forced GC, which also proves the signal types were unmapped). Greps after step 3: SemaphoreSlim, Channel<, NoInlining, GC.Collect under src/DigitalBrain.Runtime.Synapses go from 1, 1, 8, 2 occurrences to 0; BrainId under src/DigitalBrain.Runtime.Synapses goes from 45 occurrences in 4 files to UnitDeployer.cs and AllowedApis.cs only; no TryRestore or ISynapseRestorer in src (9 lines today); no RegisterState, catalog.Replace or catalog.Merge outside SynapseCatalogSource and TestOS.Register; exactly one UnitResolver.Resolve on each runtime's activation path. The retargeted SynapseHostFacts, SourceRestoreFacts, SourceBindingsFacts and SynapseHostSiloFacts stay green through the unchanged ISynapseHost port.

Your decision

1. Is proven-before-return unload a product requirement? This design makes unload eventual (context.Unload() plus Lingering()) and deletes Leaked, RetryUnload, the forced-GC loops and all 8 NoInlining helpers; if Deploy/Retire must still prove collection, UnitLoader keeps a blocking collect and about 40 lines of the old choreography return. 2. Accept that a unit stored before bindings were kept and declaring a slot is no longer refused up front (it loads; an unbound slot throws on use), and that RebuildRequired shows in List only after a failed load? 3. Accept that redeploying an identical build for an alias whose ask was never answered raises the ask again (today: Unchanged)?

Review notes on this proposal

Quote check. All 4 pairs / 15 before-fragments were opened at HEAD a275f82 and compared line by line: SynapseHost.cs 30-41, 523-528, 548-560, 680-695, 721-723, 760-766, 779-783, 859-869; SourcesNeuron.cs 59; NeuronHostGrainBase.cs 44-48; GateRunner.cs 109-118; InstallsNeuron.cs 139-149, 219-224; OrleansSynapseRegistrar.cs 35-39; StateNeuron.cs 60-66. All are verbatim with correct ranges; nothing had to be fixed. wc -l of the 23 src and 13 test files cited also matched.

Corrections the reviewer made

  • SynapseCatalogSource.cs (92) is NOT deleted: TestOS.Register/Unregister and LiveCatalogFacts.cs:59 mutate it. It shrinks by 39 counted lines and gains Of; UnitLoader no longer implements ISynapseCatalogSource (it exposes IUnitLoader.CatalogOf). Whole-file deletion total corrected from 1,605 to 1,455 in 11 files; public types removed 12 -> 11; TestOS.Source is kept.
  • ISourceStore.cs: SourceRecord with docs is 22 lines (3-24), not 11; measured deletion is 36 lines, not 58.
  • Counts fixed: SourceUsedElsewhere has 5 call sites + 1 definition (not 6 uses); restore-on-miss sites are six (TestGates.cs:117 added); proof-grep baselines re-measured (BrainId 45 occurrences in 4 files; the old 'no BrainId in Load/' was already true today).
  • Step 2 corrected: bind-once in the StateNeuron constructor would break LiveCatalogFacts.ANeuronActivatedBeforeARegisterSeesTheNewSynapses; changed to alias-at-construction + Of(typeof(TState)) per turn, and the 3 other AliasIn(_catalog.Current) sites (258, 295, 360) are covered. Removed the unnecessary 'StateNeuronFacts constructors retargeted'.
  • Step 2 proof moved from TestOS to a silo fact: TestSynapseRegistrar.cs:19 drops every host of the alias for all brains and keys, Orleans reloads only {brain}/{alias}/default, so TestOS cannot reproduce D1's silent half or D5. Recorded as an additional runtime drift.
  • Steps 3 and 4 merged into one breaking PR: the '20-line temporary shim' over the process-wide registrar cannot keep the four cross-brain SynapseHostFacts green, because the refcount compensation the step deletes is what makes the registrar correct across brains.
  • Signal-type wiring fixed: the draft registered unit [Signal] types on Loaded and never unregistered them, which would pin every collectible context in the process-wide SignalTypeMap. Unloading now carries the types (raised outside the lock) and the host unmaps them.
  • IUnitDeployment.Refuse declared [OneWayCall]: as an ordinary call from the resolver it deadlocks when the resolve runs inside the deployment's own call chain (Promote -> Rewire -> Resolve).
  • Promote reordered: dropped aliases are retired before the promote Save. After it the alias no longer resolves, the state grain cannot activate, the hook throws, InstallsNeuron.Retire swallows it (191-196) and UnregisterSchedules is skipped.
  • Propose tail guarded against a null stage after the awaited installs; OnActivate promotes a ready stage left by a crash between Save(Propose) and Promote.
  • InstallsNeuron approved branch now denies as superseded when the proposed build's needs hash differs from the asked one; without it the second half of D2 (consent recorded for other needs) survives the refactoring.
  • LiveBindings rekeyed from (brain, alias) to (brain, sourceKey) and fed on every silo from Deployed/Undeployed (marked ASSUMPTION); removes the alias loops and covers dropped aliases.
  • Host grain after-code: handle released when the rest of OnActivateAsync throws; the leave check is placed in the existing Drain() (341-344), which was marked as an unverified assumption by the designer; platform-caller stamping noted as ASSUMPTION with the BrokerSlotResolver precedent.
  • The four routing sites that throw on a registry miss were opened and named (OrleansInvocationSink.cs:57, SynapseEngine.cs:336, OrleansInstallHooks.cs:75, GateRunner.cs:237) plus INeuronHostCatalog.HostType; added the IsInterleaved round-trip cost and its fix to step 3.
  • Missed callers added to breaks/steps: AllowedApis.RefusedDigitalBrainTypes + UnitSurfaceFacts (unit boundary), UnitScenario.cs:145 (public testing API used by os tests), 5 Leaked assertions, NeuronTypeRegistry.cs:55 / TestOS built-in registration of UnitDeployment, 4 IInstalls test fakes.
  • os impact corrected: catalog.Current is read as a whole catalog at BrainEventsEndpoints.cs:91 and DebuggerReadsNeuron.cs:84/114, so os needs the brain's unit catalogs (UnitResolver.Catalogs added); os net goes from about -6 to about +20. LegacyRestoreFacts' two RebuildRequired facts are dropped.
  • Tests re-classified: SynapseCatalogSourceFacts trimmed (not deleted); SourceBindingsFlipFacts is a rewrite (hand-made fakes); net tests about -80, not -190. src adds raised from 946 to 1,045 est.; net src about -760, not -930.
  • Overstatement removed: 'Fixes D1 fully' -> code and state only; step for per-brain signal types made required (not optional) for units that declare signals. D3 reworded (best-effort not verified). D6 reworded to what is actually guaranteed.
  • Defect added: D9 - after a restart an update that changes needs bypasses staging (SynapseHost.cs:178 requires an in-memory entry) and overwrites the stored record.
  • Behaviour changes the designer buried are now explicit in breaks and openDecision: RebuildRequired only after a failed resolve; the 'no stored bindings + slot' refusal rule disappears.
  • Pair 1 gained DeploymentState.Refuse (called by pairs 2 and 3); [ReadOnlyCall] removed from ISynapseHost.Source (a DI port, not a neuron contract); ISynapseHost.Source reads a dedicated IUnitDeployment.Source instead of pulling the whole StoredBuild with bytes.

Doubts that remain

  • Not verified: that Save and outgoing neuron calls work inside Neuron.OnActivate (the grain sets _neuron and loads synapse state only after NeuronAttachment.Activate, NeuronHostGrainBase.cs:58-65). The legacy drain and promote-on-activate depend on it; if it fails they move to the first non-read-only call.
  • Not verified: caller identity. The resolver's reads of synapse.sources and synapse.deployment run inside another neuron's activation under the activating caller's RequestContext, and UnitDeployment calls IInstalls as a neuron where SynapseHost called it from DI with no caller. If access rules gate these contracts by caller, the delegates must stamp the brain's platform as BrokerSlotResolver.cs:28-30 does.
  • Not verified: whether brain-wide edge deliveries interleave with a suspended turn. The Propose tail is written to be safe either way.
  • Not verified: IClusterListeners delivers Deployed/Undeployed to every silo. If not, convergence of already active grains on other silos is 'when idle', and a grain that is never idle keeps the old build.
  • Not verified: a source key is always a legal neuron key (os uses the alias as source key, e.g. 'watch.web'); a key containing '/' would not parse as brain/type/key.
  • Not verified: journal/grain-state size limits for a DeploymentState holding two images, and whether each Save appends the full state to the journal.
  • The three journal-side signal decode sites (BrainSignalLog.cs:135, BrainJournalWriter.cs:171, BrainSignalHistory.cs:83) were not opened; step 5's size is the designer's claim.
  • The stand-in class for interleave binding of unit calls assumes NeuronHostGrainBase.Bind and NeutralCallMapping only read contract methods common to every StateNeuron<T>; without it each unit call pays an extra IsInterleaved round trip.
  • R03's exact footprint (GateRunner.cs:237, TestGates.cs:117, GateChanged in InstallsNeuron) is taken from the brief, not re-checked.
  • D2, D3, D4, D9 are read from code paths, not reproduced; whether production ever runs more than one silo or more than one brain per alias decides how often D1/D6 actually bite.

R14 · Simulation · wave 1

Name the simulator; test adapters once, not hosts twice

Move the simulator (42 files, 6,094 lines) out of DigitalBrain.Testing into an xunit-free DigitalBrain.Simulation package that the os deploy gate references, replace the fakes that have their own semantics by a NeuronHarness over the simulator, add a differential fact that runs a unit's scenarios on the simulator and on a real silo and compares transcripts, and, once the kernel is one copy, replace the 13 hand-copied *SiloFacts and the 8-fact host kit by a 6-fact adapter smoke that every topology runs.

Net
src about -130 (-931 measured, +803 est.) / db tests about -1,795 (-2,125 measured, +330 est.; about -1,680 if R03 already removed its 114 gate-fact lines) / os tests about +20 (-72 measured, +90 est.) with 11 files (66 facts) rewritten in place; os production 1 csproj line and 1 PackageVersion line. The gain is not src lines: it is 1,913 lines of twin tests gone and xunit out of production.
Effort
L (1-2 weeks, nearer two): a 42-file package split with public-API bookkeeping, a silo scenario host with two unverified seams to spike, a 6-fact smoke on three topologies, and two os bumps, the first of which rewrites 66 facts in 11 files by hand.
Depends on
Steps 1-4 (package split, differential on today's types, harness, first os bump) depend on nothing
R04 (journal port and its JournalConformance kit on both adapters): gate for step 5
The move that puts subscriptions in the journal with one Position and replaces the stream by a one-way nudge (called R06 in R04's text and R05 in this move's design; its design was not in the folder): gives Settle and the silo Idle() a real definition; gate for step 5
R03 : removes 114 gate-fact lines inside the 13 twin files before step 5 deletes the rest
R02 : closes the pure-rule drifts the differential would otherwise report as skipped cases
Review confidence
medium

What is wrong today

DigitalBrain.Testing is 53 files / 8,523 lines; only 7 files (2,020 lines) use xunit, yet the package references xunit.v3.assert (DigitalBrain.Testing.csproj:20-21) and one os PRODUCTION project references it (IntoChat.OS.Modules.Apps.csproj:12), because the deploy gate for AI-authored units is UnitScenario.Run -> TestOS.Local() (UnitScenario.cs:52; called at os BuilderTools.cs:81 and ScenarioTranscripts.cs:35). The simulator is kept equal to Orleans by hand: 13 *SiloFacts files (1,913 lines, 42 facts, 43 sleeps or polls) re-type simulator facts (NeuronCheckFacts and NeuronCheckSiloFacts share 33 byte-identical fixture lines; the two InstallEdges twins with one fact name assert 5 and 12), and a host kit of 6 files / 588 lines with 8 facts whose wait helper returns silently after 45 s (HostConformanceFacts.cs:23) and whose two hardest facts are skipped on the consumer's real composition (os IntoChatOsTarget.cs:71 and :98, 'Pre-existing DigitalBrain master failure ... deferred to 0.13 cleanup'; os pins 0.18). On a silo Idle() is Task.CompletedTask and time is Task.Delay (BothRuntimesFixture.cs:143-145), so no test can run a unit's scenarios (9 tick steps in the 10 shipped units) on the runtime the unit is deployed to. A third context, FakeNeuronContext (Fakes.cs:507), has other semantics again (state by reference with no codec, a History nothing writes to, timers that reach no neuron, no turns) and is used by 2 db test files and 11 os test files (66 facts). os tests also reach into the simulator's privates by reflection (LegacyRestoreFacts.cs:72,208; DeployFlowFacts.cs:300), which every kernel move will break at run time.

Design principle

A bounded context that production depends on is a product and must be named and packaged as one, not hidden in a test kit. A driven port is specified by one executable contract every adapter passes (R04's JournalConformance), and a rule that lives in one kernel is tested once; what remains to test per host is only that its adapters are wired, plus one anti-corruption check for the deploy gate: the same scenario, two hosts, equal transcripts.

Defects this removes

  • xunit ships inside an os production module: src/DigitalBrain.Testing/DigitalBrain.Testing.csproj:20-21 reached through os IntoChat.OS.Modules.Apps.csproj:12.
  • Two of the eight host-conformance facts are skipped on the os composition as a 'pre-existing master failure ... deferred to 0.13 cleanup': os IntoChatOsTarget.cs:71 (Gap) and :98 (kill between write and publish).
  • A kit wait that gives up silently: src/DigitalBrain.Testing.Silo/Conformance/Host/HostConformanceFacts.cs:23 returns when the 45 s deadline passes, so a fact that never converges fails later on an unrelated assertion.
  • Idle() is a no-op on the silo: src/DigitalBrain.Testing.Silo/BothRuntimesFixture.cs:143 and Conformance/Host/OrleansRun.cs:67; 37 os call sites of WaitForSignals (11 files) poll because of it. Fixed in step 5, when the journal can say who is behind.
  • A fake history nothing writes to: src/DigitalBrain.Testing/Fakes.cs:563 with :516 and :586; a neuron under FakeNeuronContext that reads Context.History after its own Save gets an empty page.
  • A fake timer that reaches no neuron: src/DigitalBrain.Testing/NeuronHarness.cs:15 wires only Reminders.Target; Fakes.cs:120 calls OnFire, which is null unless the fact sets it.
  • A fake state that skips the wire codec: src/DigitalBrain.Testing/Fakes.cs:55 (_value = next) against src/DigitalBrain.Testing/TestOS/TestJournal.cs:113.
  • Consumer tests coupled to the simulator's private fields: os LegacyRestoreFacts.cs:72 and :208 (TestJournal._states by reflection) and DeployFlowFacts.cs:300 (TestJournal.Save by reflection; its comment at :298 says no public seed exists, while TestOS.cs:330 is one). They compile through any rewrite of the journal and fail at run time.
  • Same-named twin facts that assert different things: tests/DigitalBrain.Testing.Tests.Unit/TestOS/InstallEdgesFacts.cs:78 asserts 5, tests/DigitalBrain.Runtime.Orleans.Tests.Integration/Synapses/InstallEdgesSiloFacts.cs:57 asserts 12; NeuronCheckSiloFacts.cs:68 has an assertion its original lacks.
  • No test runs a unit's scenarios on the runtime it is deployed to (UnitScenario.cs:52 is the only host). ScenarioDifferentialFacts (db) and ShippedUnitsDifferentialFacts (os, all 10 shipped units) do.
  • Drift D15 (slot bindings are wrapped by interceptors on Orleans only, BrokerSlotResolver.cs:54) is covered by no fact today; SiloSmoke.ASlotResolvesThroughTheBrokerInterceptors pins the Orleans side.

A fact typed twice becomes one kernel fact and one adapter fact

The 33 lines above each 'before' fact are byte-identical in the two files (diff prints nothing); they move once into the kit's neuron file (FrameworkNeurons.cs, 133 lines, already Orleans-free, kept) as ISmokeCounter. The journal assertions of the silo copy belong to the journal port's kit, which R04 owns (JournalConformance, 15 facts, both adapters); this move does not write a second one. The smoke takes an IInProcessHost and nothing else: the 9 abstract controls of RunningTarget (fault hooks, clock, checkpoint) are not needed, so os can run it on its own composition without the two Assert.Skip it has today.

Fakes with their own semantics become one harness over the simulator

The fake keeps the state object by reference and never touches the codec; its History is a separate list that Save and Publish never append to; NeuronHarness.cs:15 wires reminders but not timers. A neuron that passes on it can fail on both runtimes. Deleted: ManualTime, InMemoryNeuronState, ManualTimers, ManualReminders, RecordingSynapses, FakeNeuronProxies, FakeCapabilityBroker, FakeNeuronContext. Kept: FakeOperations (it runs the shared StorageWriteRunner and the simulator uses it, TestNeuronContext.cs:25) and InMemorySignalHistory (a correct standalone ISignalHistory used by one db and one os endpoint test). TestClock stays: swapping it for FakeTimeProvider would save 89 lines and put a package named '...TimeProvider.Testing' into the os production closure, with firing semantics nobody ran. Seed also replaces the three os reflection sites into TestJournal's privates.

xunit leaves the production deploy gate; the scenario loop takes a host

The split is a file move with no dependency on any kernel move, so it goes first. The only back-references from the 42 moved files to the 11 kept ones are six doc crefs (InMemoryIdentity.cs:8, InMemorySecrets.cs:7, InMemoryStorage.cs:13, StorageSeed.cs:13, TestOS/Apps/IInstalledApp.cs:4), which must be reworded in the same commit. os compiles from source against both assemblies and the namespaces stay, so no os source file changes; type forwarders are insurance for already-compiled consumers, not a requirement. The architecture anchor typeof(DigitalBrain.Testing.ManualTime) (FrameworkFacts.cs:98, HttpBoundaryFacts.cs:44) would silently resolve to the wrong assembly after the move and must change to a type that stays.

A silo that cannot say it is idle becomes a differential transcript fact

Today the silo's Idle is a no-op and its time is Task.Delay, the kit's Until returns silently after 45 s, 37 os call sites poll with WaitForSignals, and os skips two of the eight kit facts on its own composition. The differential needs no fault hook and no twin: one scenario, two hosts, one text. It runs on the DigitalBrain local topology, so it proves the Orleans adapters, not os's own interceptors. VirtualReminders (est. 70 lines, a sorted table of grain, name, due and period behind IReminderRegistry) and the pinned clock are the two unverified seams; if the registry cannot be replaced, AdvanceTo fires ReceiveReminder for the schedules the time neuron lists instead.

Deleted lines

What breaks

  • FakeNeuronContext (public) deleted: 2 db test files (ConfirmationRuntimeFacts.cs 2 constructions, OperationRecoveryFacts.cs 5) and 11 os test files (9 constructions, 13 mentions, 66 facts: Cloud Auth, Lobby, Mail, Organisations; Gmail x2, Azure, GitHub, Mcp x2, Salesforce). os calls NeuronAttachment.Attach itself and then the neuron instance directly; each fact moves to harness.Run.
  • InMemoryNeuronState, ManualTimers, ManualReminders, RecordingSynapses, FakeNeuronProxies (public) deleted: 1 db test file (TestingFacts.cs), 0 os. FakeCapabilityBroker: 2 db test files, 0 os. ManualTime: 4 db test files (2 are architecture anchors), 0 os.
  • NeuronHarness<T>.Neuron, .Context and Deactivate(cause) removed: 1 db test file, 0 os.
  • Host kit types (HostConformanceFacts, FrameworkTarget, OrleansFrameworkTarget, RunningTarget, FrameworkHostOptions, OrleansRun, OffsetClock, TestOSTarget, LocalOrleansTarget) deleted: 4 db test files (FrameworkHostConformanceFacts.cs, AzuriteOrleansTarget.cs, BrainActivationFacts.cs, SandboxConformanceFacts.cs), 1 architecture pin (FrameworkFacts.cs:41) and 2 os files (OsHostConformanceFacts.cs 23 lines, IntoChatOsTarget.cs 137 lines). FrameworkModule and the kit neurons keep their names; their namespace changes to DigitalBrain.Testing.Conformance.Smoke (2 os using lines).
  • Package split: 0 source breaks in os. os production changes 1 line (IntoChat.OS.Modules.Apps.csproj:12) and 1 line in Directory.Packages.props. 48 os projects reference DigitalBrain.Testing or .Silo; exactly 1 is not a test or test-kit project.
  • UnitScenario.Run(source, fakes, steps, ct) keeps its signature: the 2 os production call sites (BuilderTools.cs:81, ScenarioTranscripts.cs:35) do not change.
  • TestOS.Clock stays a TestClock: the 29 os uses (10 Advance, 19 GetUtcNow) do not change.
  • Kept and not broken: InMemorySignalHistory (db JournalTailEndpointFacts.cs, os LocalOwnerJournalTailFacts.cs), FakeOperations, BothRuntimesFixture / INeuronRuntime / NeuronRuntimeKind (15 / 19 / 36 os files, the app template, the Calculator example), ModuleOnTestOSFacts (25 os files and the os scaffolder template), AppBaseline (13), BddRunner (12), SiloHost (11), the four capability kits.
  • Rename, only if taken: TestOS in db 44 src + 55 test + 7 template/example/doc files and in os 72 files (16 use it as a declared type); TestJournal in 2 os files, both of which reflect into its privates and must already be on Seed after step 4; TestBrain in 1 db test file and 1 os string literal.
  • New package id DigitalBrain.Simulation in the NuGet cascade. No new third-party dependency. No persisted data changes.

Risks

  • Replacing Orleans.Timers.IReminderRegistry in the silo's services is unverified (orleans-facts.md confirms only that the registry exists and that reminders arrive at IRemindable.ReceiveReminder). Step 2 spikes it before anything depends on it. Fallback: AdvanceTo fires ReceiveReminder for the schedules the time neuron lists, which re-implements the due rule in about 30 lines.
  • A silo whose GetUtcNow stands still is unverified; the only precedent is OffsetClock, which jumps forward (HostConformanceFacts.cs:232). If Orleans misbehaves, the clock becomes a settable offset and TranscriptText drops the instant except Tick.At.
  • Settle by silence (step 2 form) is a heuristic: a reaction slower than 300 ms reads as quiet and the case fails with a missing event. It is replaced in step 5; until then a flaky case is a wait problem first, a drift second.
  • TranscriptText compares per publisher because the order between two publishers reacting to one signal is not deterministic on a silo. A unit whose fold depends on that order shows up as a flaky case. That is a real property of the unit on Orleans that the simulator's single order hides; the differential makes it visible, it does not fix it.
  • The differential runs on the DigitalBrain local topology with fakes bound by slot name. It does not exercise os's own capability interceptors, policies or cloud profile; the per-topology smoke is what touches those.
  • Deleting 42 silo facts before the kernel is one copy would remove cover that exists nowhere else. Step 5 is gated on R04's kit being green on both adapters and on the differential running without a skipped case.
  • Step 4 rewrites 66 os facts by hand in a bump that is also a production deploy. Production behaviour does not change, but the bump is large; it can be split by module because the old fakes only disappear from os at the bump.
  • Public-API bookkeeping: RS0016/RS0017 are errors in the split packages, and how the analyzer wants forwarded types listed was not checked. It fails at build time, not at run time. Dropping Forwarders.cs is safe for os (it compiles from source) and removes this risk.
  • R04 places its journal kit in Testing.Silo and its subclasses in the Orleans integration project, which does not reference Testing.Silo today (csproj lines 3-6). That reference has to be added by R04; this move then moves ApprovingConfirmations there instead of copying it.

Proof it worked

1) After step 4: grep over os csproj files finds no project outside *.Tests.* and *.Testing that references DigitalBrain.Testing or DigitalBrain.Testing.Silo (today exactly 1 does); the new os architecture fact asserts it. 2) TestTierFacts.SimulationShipsNoTestFramework is green and the CoreHasNoOrleans facts are green for DigitalBrain.Simulation. 3) grep -rw 'FakeNeuronContext\|ManualTimers\|ManualReminders\|RecordingSynapses\|FakeNeuronProxies\|FakeCapabilityBroker' over both repos returns nothing; grep -rn 'typeof(TestJournal)' over os returns nothing; Fakes.cs holds only InMemorySignalHistory and FakeOperations. 4) The two spike facts of step 2 are green, or the fallback is in place. 5) ScenarioDifferentialFacts (db, 4 fixture sources) and ShippedUnitsDifferentialFacts (os, 10 units, every kept scenario including the 9 tick steps) pass with no Skip. 6) After step 5: find tests -name '*SiloFacts.cs' returns nothing; src/DigitalBrain.Testing.Silo/Conformance/Host does not exist; SiloSmoke's 6 facts pass on the local topology, on Azurite and on the os composition with no Assert.Skip in IntoChatOsTarget.cs; the count of 'Task.Delay|Until(' lines in Runtime.Orleans.Tests.Integration is below today's 144 by at least the 43 in the deleted files. 7) R04's JournalConformance is green on both adapters (its proof, this move's gate).

Your decision

1) Do the 37 os test files that run module facts on both runtimes (BothRuntimesFixture; also in the app template and the Calculator example, and written into os CLAUDE.md as the Integration tier) keep doing so after one kernel? Recommended: no; simulator facts plus the per-topology smoke, then delete the 236-line fixture (step 8). Alternative: keep the fixture as a supported consumer tool with a real Idle(). 2) Rename the class TestOS to SimulatedBrain (step 7: 72 os files, mechanical, one commit per repo, TestBrain deleted in the same commit), or keep the name TestOS inside the new package? The package split and everything else here work either way. The first attempt (commit 904abcc) left both names; if it is done, it must be done whole.

Review notes on this proposal

Quote check. 13 of 13 'before' fragments checked against the files at HEAD (digitalbrain a275f82, os 5c18b3eb5): all verbatim at the stated line ranges; no text needed fixing. Edits to the quotes in the final: pair 1 elides the listener block of the silo copy (lines 51-55) with a '// ...' line; pair 3 drops TestTierFacts.cs:70-73 because the final no longer changes that fact; pair 4 adds os IntoChatOsTarget.cs:69-72, copied from the file. The diff of the 33 fixture lines (NeuronCheckFacts.cs:7-39 against NeuronCheckSiloFacts.cs:8-40) prints nothing.

Corrections the reviewer made

  • Removed the designer's JournalConformance kit and its invented port (IJournal, CommitResult.Committed/Conflict, Subscribe/Behind/Trim, MemoryJournal(brain, clock, options), BrainLogJournal.Open, IJournals). R04's design now exists and already owns the journal port (internal IBrainJournal, IBrainJournals, ISignalNudge, Commit with CursorMove and Stamp, StaleVersionException) and a 15-fact JournalConformance<TTarget> kit; a second kit with different shapes would not compile against it.
  • Removed NudgeConformance and TimersConformance: 13 facts with empty bodies or comments (they would pass vacuously), and on the Orleans side both need a running host. Replaced by a 6-fact SiloSmoke over IInProcessHost; the two facts shown are real code on types that exist today (IClusterListeners.Listen, SiloNeurons.Get, ISignalHistory.Watch, IInProcessHost.Restart).
  • Removed the 'Azure journal x1' kit subclass: DigitalBrain.Runtime.Orleans.Azure has no journal adapter (4 files, 582 lines: blob-lease ownership, cloud profile, storage account). Cloud coverage is kept by running the smoke on Azurite.
  • Dropped the FakeTimeProvider swap. It saved 89 lines, put a package named '...TimeProvider.Testing' into the os production closure, and its firing semantics were unverified where TestClock.cs:37 is explicit. TestClock stays; ManualTime is deleted and its 4 real users move to TestClock.
  • Corrected the ManualTime census: 4 db test files use DigitalBrain.Testing.ManualTime, not 13. Nine Orleans test files use their own internal ManualTime (StorageSupport.cs:21).
  • Corrected the package cut: 42 files / 6,094 lines move and 11 files / 2,429 lines stay (the designer's summary said 46 / 6,503, contradicting their own section 4). Verified the only back-references from moved to kept files are 6 doc crefs in 5 files, and added their fix to step 1.
  • Kept InMemorySignalHistory (correct standalone ISignalHistory used by db JournalTailEndpointFacts.cs and os LocalOwnerJournalTailFacts.cs): the Fakes.cs deletion is lines 8-241 and 451-614 = 398, not 445, and two breaks disappear.
  • Kept FrameworkNeurons.cs (133 lines, already Orleans-free kit neurons): the host-kit deletion is 5 files / 455 lines, not 588.
  • Removed JournalTailFacts.cs (90) and TraceStampingConformanceFacts.cs (180) from the deletions: they run on BothRuntimesFixture, not on the host kit, and their replacement belongs to R04's kit. They move to the optional last step.
  • Corrected the R03 overlap from 'est. 450 lines' to 114 lines in 5 fact methods, measured with the skeptic's own verify/gate_tests.py; the R03 design does not mention the *SiloFacts files.
  • Added missed breaks: BrainActivationFacts.cs and SandboxConformanceFacts.cs use the host kit's targets (4 db test files break, not 2); HttpBoundaryFacts.cs:17,44 and UnitSurfaceFacts.cs:12 name the package; the architecture anchor typeof(ManualTime) would silently resolve to the wrong assembly after the split.
  • Added a missed defect: os skips 2 of the 8 host-conformance facts on its own composition (IntoChatOsTarget.cs:71 and :98, 'pre-existing master failure ... deferred to 0.13 cleanup').
  • Added a missed defect and break: os tests reflect into TestJournal's privates (LegacyRestoreFacts.cs:72,208; DeployFlowFacts.cs:300). The designer listed these as a mechanical rename. They are fixed by the new TestOS.Seed in step 4.
  • Corrected os FakeNeuronContext numbers: 11 files, 9 constructions, 13 mentions, 66 facts in 2,154 lines; os never uses NeuronHarness. Effort text for the first os bump changed from 'about 15 files' to '66 facts'.
  • Fixed TranscriptText use: it was rendered after both hosts were disposed, and RuntimeProtocol.OptionsFor caches per collectible load context until Forget, so it would pin unit assemblies. The fact now starts both hosts itself and renders before dispose.
  • Changed the theory data from TheoryData<DifferentialCase> (delegates, not serializable) to case names, following the repo's own precedent at HostConformanceFacts.cs:56-57.
  • Fixed SiloScenarioHost so a unit can actually deploy: installs need the approver double (ApprovingConfirmations, as HostStateFacts.cs:66) and slots resolve through ICapabilityConnections (as SlotResolutionSiloFacts.cs:63-78). Replaced the wrapper Deploy member by ISynapseHost, which both hosts already expose.
  • Replaced the R05-dependent Settle (journal.Behind(), IJournals) by a version on today's types (journal Last stable across six reads), stated as a heuristic, with the journal-based definition deferred to step 5.
  • Rewrote all 'after' code under the names that exist after the package split (TestOS, namespace DigitalBrain.Testing) so it does not presume the rename; the rename is now an owner decision and its last step. Changed the proposed name TestJournal -> MemoryJournal to SimulatedJournal because R04 uses MemoryJournal in the kernel.
  • Removed the text-grep architecture fact EveryPortKitRunsOnBothAdapterFamilies (it demands a Unit-tier subclass of a kit that R04 places in Testing.Silo, which Unit projects may not reference) and the body-unchanged rename of the unit-tier fact. Dropped TestTierFacts.cs:70-73 from pair 3's 'before' since nothing changes there.
  • Reordered the steps: the package split goes first (it is a file move with no kernel dependency and removes the production defect now), then the differential on today's types, then the harness; deleting the twins and the host kit stays last and gated. Step 1 of the designer was labelled 'tests only' although it adds public API to a packaged project.
  • Corrected the forwarder claim: a missing TypeForwardedTo is not a compile error in os (it compiles from source against both assemblies with unchanged namespaces). Forwarders are kept as insurance only.
  • Moved the smoke subclasses to Framework.Tests.E2E: the Orleans integration project does not reference DigitalBrain.Testing.Silo.
  • Softened overstatement: the differential proves the Orleans adapters on the DigitalBrain local topology, not os's composition; 'SiloSmokeFacts has no Task.Delay' replaced by waits that fail at the wait; the list of drifts the differential 'will' hit replaced by 'skipped with the drift number'.
  • Recomputed netLoc from the corrected lists: src about -130, db tests about -1,795, os tests about +20.

Doubts that remain

  • Nothing was built or run. The 'after' code was checked by reading the members it calls (TestOS.cs:58, 330, 544; TestNeuronHost.cs:81, 222; ISignalHistory.Watch; SiloNeurons.Get; LocalSiloHostFactory; BrainStorageRegistration; ISynapseHost), not by a compiler.
  • Replacing Orleans.Timers.IReminderRegistry through the host's service callback is an assumption; orleans-facts.md does not confirm it, and neither does it say anything about TimeProvider inside a silo.
  • A silo on a clock whose GetUtcNow stands still has no precedent in the repo (OffsetClock only jumps forward).
  • The design of the subscriptions/nudge move was not in the proposals folder, so the journal-based Settle of step 5 has no concrete shape yet; R04 and the designer also disagree on its number (R06 against R05).
  • Whether every one of the 66 os facts can drive its neuron through harness.Run was not checked fact by fact; some call the instance outside any turn today.
  • ScenarioModule hosting ScenarioInput on a silo assumes a module in the Simulation assembly needs no manifest entries beyond what FrameworkModule has; ScenarioInput is the only [Neuron] class in the assembly today (measured), so no alias collides.
  • How the public-API analyzer wants forwarded types listed was not checked; dropping Forwarders.cs avoids the question.
  • TheoryData<string>(IEnumerable<string>) and Theory(Timeout) on xunit v3 are used from memory of the API, not from a file in the repo; the params form is what the repo uses today.
  • The four smoke facts not shown (deployed source and view, slot through interceptors, alias reaction as JSON, reminder across deactivation) are estimates of 20 lines each; the reminder one needs ReminderOptions.MinimumReminderPeriod lowered as StatefulSilo does (HostStateFacts.cs:65).
  • The claim that the 11 kept files of DigitalBrain.Testing compile against the moved types rests on a grep of the TestOS members they call plus an InternalsVisibleTo; it was not compiled.

Defects found on the way

41 defects, found by reading

None of these was reproduced by running code. "read" means an agent walked the code path and quoted it. Several are worth fixing this week whatever happens to the refactoring: the os silo that runs the access policy with zero stages, the interface-level [Internal] exemption, the retention floor that ignores live cursors, and the os neuron that writes a framework backing field by its compiler-generated name.

Your decisions

Eight questions only the owner can answer

Each proposal lists its own smaller decisions. These are the ones that change the shape of the work.

  1. Is a veto by a third party a product requirement? The gate lets one unit refuse another neuron's signal. If no, delete it as R03 proposes; a cap is already served by the Policies reservation, which fails safe where the gate fails open. If yes, keep the check but evaluate it inside the journal append. Count installed gate synapses in production brains before either.
  2. Is an out-of-process client still a commitment? os removed its sandbox lane on 8 November and nothing in production dispatches a runtime/v1 call. Dropping the client and edge takes another 1,219 source lines out (R01).
  3. May saving with no signal become an error? Twelve production sites do it today by reaching around the base class. R04 gives them an explicit member and asks to refuse the empty list one release later.
  4. Which thing keeps the word "Synapse"? R08 keeps it for the subscription edge and renames the functions to [Reaction] and [View]. R09 keeps it for the function and renames the edge to Subscription. The two proposals disagree; choose once, before either rename starts.
  5. May first-party screen state become stored state? R10 makes each app a neuron, so 14 state records and their views are written to the journal on every interaction. If that is not acceptable, the App model, its analyzers and its test kit (about 3,250 source lines) move to os beside their host.
  6. A typed lane for build-time neurons. Not now. Run HostCostFacts first; there is no evidence yet that six JSON passes per call matter.
  7. One silo per brain. Both shipped topologies rely on it, but only the storage lease enforces it. Say whether it is a rule.
  8. Retention. The docs promise the floor never passes a live cursor. In production the lookup always returns null. Wire it (R05) or withdraw the promise.

Method and limits

How this was produced, and what it cannot claim

Three passes

  1. Map. Sixteen readers, one per subsystem plus metrics, the os consumer view, Orleans 10 documentation and project history. Each read its files in full and quoted code with line numbers.
  2. Try to refute. Six skeptics re-measured each part of the thesis and corrected it. Three advocates argued three target architectures and a judge scored them.
  3. Design and review. One designer per proposal wrote the code. One critic per proposal re-opened the files and fixed the proposal.

Limits

  • Read-only. No build, no test run, no silo start. After-code was reviewed and never compiled.
  • Eight proposals were designed in a second run after a usage limit stopped the first. All fourteen were then reviewed. The later ones were told to reuse the type names of the earlier ones (Commit, Position, Caller); the names were not cross-checked by a single reader.
  • Every "after" size and every net figure is an estimate. "Today" figures are line counts.
  • The skeptics corrected the first-pass thesis in several places. The main ones: the tunnel is small; the silo has one base model; fossils are 5% of the source; identity is carried both ambiently and explicitly, and the fault is that the two can disagree.
  • A grep of shipped code cannot prove that no installed brain declares a gate. That needs the census in move 1.
